# Graph Report - itc-020  (2026-11-02)

## Corpus Check
- 5301 files · ~6,194,546 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 33 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 13, .css 6, .csv 4)

## Summary
- 59037 nodes · 187660 edges · 1493 communities (1209 shown, 284 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 88% EXTRACTED · 12% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 22802 edges (avg confidence: 0.94)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `f4dc5dd2`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
